3b1(sher phyin): dang tha snyad tsam du zad do//byang chub dmigs su med pa nyid kyi phyir 'di lta ste/byang chub ces bya ba 'di ni ming dang tha snyad tsam du zad de/'di lta ste/yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas shes bya ba 'di ni dmigs su med pa nyid kyis phyir/ming

3b3(sher phyin): sems dang po bskyed pa nas bzung ste/nyams pa med pa'i mngon par shes pa lnga yongs su rdzogs par byed/sems can rnams yongs su smin par byed/sangs rgyas kyi zhing yang yongs su dag par byed/sangs rgyas kyis zhing nas sangs rgyas 
3b4(sher phyin): kyi zhing du 'gro zhing /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams la bkur sti byed/bla mar byed/ri mor byed/mchod pa byed cing sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/de dag las yang dang yang du 'di lta ste/byang chub sems dpa'i theg pa rab mchog gi theg

3b7(sher phyin): pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par sangs ma rgyas kyi bar du nam yang theg pa de dang bral bar mi 'gyur ro//de rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid rjes su thob nas dge sbyong ngam/bram ze 'am/lha 'am/bdud dam/tshangs pa 'am/gzhan sus 
3b8(sher phyin): kyang ma bskor ba'i chos kyi 'khor lo bskor bar 'gyur ro//shar phyogs kyi 'jig rten gyi khams gang gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed dag na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das gang dag 'tsho zhing bzhugs pa dang /de bzhin du phyogs mtshams kyi bar gyi 'jig 
4a1(sher phyin): @#//rten gyi khams gang gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed dag na 'tsho zhing bzhugs pa de dag kyang 'jig rten gyi khams che ge mo zhig na/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po che ge mo zhig theg pa chen po la yang dag par zhon nas/rnam pa
